- **tree**: `tree` renders a flat source as a nested hierarchy. Every row carries its own id (`idcol`, default `id`) and the id of its parent (`parentidcol`, default `parentid`); rows whose parent id equals `nullvalue` (default `"0"`) are roots. Each root is rendered with a face, its children are rendered recursively and inserted at the face's `@child` placeholder, and so on until a row has no children.
